### 2026-10-16 Diagnose pipeline failures in scribe and secretary

- **Session:** host (d7e03a48-93bd-44ef-a1d2-045ad84afabf)

(2) Dropped jobs: con finishing while scribe busy exits silently within 30–60s window, never retried; dispatcher to queue dropped jobs instead. (3) Secretary reword stakes cause infinite retry loops (guard rejects edit, re-queues unchanged task); secretary to refuse reword, accept amend-item stakes. (4) Secretary brief still lists TD-nn format; update brief to slug format. (5) No rejection log for debugging; add log of what failed edits tried to change. Memory file added: never ask backs to reword.

- **Learned:** Hand-off truncation cuts Friction without marker. Dropped jobs need queue; currently no detection. Reword stakes loop: con re-edits item, guard rejects as "changed by con", task re-queues with no edit applied.
- **Decisions:** Keep full con hand-off (never truncate Friction/For repo sections). Queue dropped jobs instead of silent exit. Secretary refuses reword stakes, accepts amend-item stakes. Update secretary brief to slug format. Engineer to stake amend items for re-edits, never ask backs to reword.

- **Came out:** Root cause found: test commands ran real git binary in scratch folder, bypassing wrapper that normally isolates GIT_DIR/GIT_WORK_TREE from shared repo config. This set core.filemode to mode 100755 and core.worktree to container path, breaking Windows checkout. Repair procedure documented: 7-step command to restore core.filemode, unset core.worktree, reset branches, and clean stray test commits. Pending Operator execution.
- **Friction:** [tooling] wrapper bypass not prevented or detected during subprocess spawn. [env] GIT_DIR and GIT_WORK_TREE set globally, bypassed by process outside wrapper shell.
- **Learned:** Wrapper design prevents exactly this failure; test isolation required when GIT_* variables set globally in environment.
- **Decisions:** none
- **For the repository:** [docs] For tests using git in scratch repos, isolate container environment with env -u GIT_DIR -u GIT_WORK_TREE.
- **Decisions needed:** (1) Use git worktrees instead of GIT_DIR pattern for scratch test repos? (2) Add guard to detect unset GIT_WORK_TREE in shared repo config?

## Review 2026-10-20

- **Entries reviewed:** 29, from "2026-10-19 PR #13 retargeted to main, mergeable after prior failure" to "2026-10-20 Feature collection release validated on GHCR"; none corrected. Read by the host. The entries for the review's own work, after these, open the next review.
- **Costs (costs-2026-10-operator.md):** $49.84 at list price from 2026-10-19 to 2026-10-20 16:26 in the committed report: host (Opus) $35.98 in 2 sessions, Sonnet cons $4.20 in 8 runs (about $0.53 a run), backs $9.66 in 185 runs, 1 undone. After the rebuild the report regenerated as $2.21 (group 1); with the fix it reads $55.19 for the month. Host stretches ran from $0.16 to $6.55; the large ones were writing the principles with the Operator.
- **Recurrence:** `scribe-cost-deduplication` came back (11 of 29 entries repeat their stretch's cost; the job wrote it onto every entry by design): done now, group 2. `guard-corrects-none` came back (one new `Corrects: none`): done now, group 3. `scribe-commit-verification` came back in kind ("PR 19 is blocked by checks" when all 69 passed and a review was required): kept open. Held: the stale-install warning; the install that forced `--no-verify` on #19 was 1.2.3, older than the warning, and is 1.4.0 now (`devhost-install-current` ticked).
- **Groups and actions:**
  1. The cost report lost its history on a rebuild: it was rebuilt from the volumes' transcripts and run records only, so $49.84 became $2.21 and the briefing read $0.00. Changed now: the report keeps a committed store, `costs-YYYY-MM-<engineer>.jsonl` (ids and numbers), merges this machine's records into it, and carries this month's earlier figures as a baseline taken from the committed report; tests `test_a_rebuild_loses_no_history`, `test_a_baseline_adds_to_every_table` (devhost 1.4.1).
  2. A stretch's cost on every entry it produced: changed now in the scribe job, the cost on the first entry and a pointer on the others; functional check "the stretch's cost is on its first entry only" (closes `scribe-cost-deduplication`).
  3. Claims the scribe did not check (`Corrects: none`, "blocked by checks", "volumes preserve the Claude session", which held for the session but not its transcript history): `guard-corrects-none` done now, the ledger guard refuses the line from 2026-10-19 and the job drops it, tests `test_corrects_none_is_refused_from_2026_10_19_only` and a functional check; the one uncommitted line of 2026-10-20 removed before its first commit. `scribe-commit-verification` stays open.
  4. GitHub writes from the container (gh not logged in, no git credentials, the `!` prompt cannot finish an interactive login, auto mode refusing a remote branch delete): `gh-auth-briefing`. The refused delete gets no permission rule: it cannot be undone, and the Operator running it is the right gate.
  5. Context from a claude.ai session lost to this one (unreadable from the container; arrived as a pasted snippet): `cloud-session-handoff`.
  6. Documents drift: the co-author trailer named Sonnet while sessions run Opus, changed now (CLAUDE.md names the model that wrote the commit; the `close-session` template matches the repository's skill); the stale journalist references: `journalist-refs-reconcile`.
  7. Decisions left in entries and never filed (arthurian theme in the foundation, `devhost-package` and missing foundation entries, SETUP's merge or replace): folded into `open-questions-batch`, still open from the last review.
  8. Models: no tier moved. The large Opus stretches were judgment work with the Operator; the Sonnet cons at about $0.53 a run wrote skills and docs that cannot be checked mechanically; the backs cost $0.05 to $0.13 a run, the librarian highest per change ($0.79).
  9. Shell retries (a zsh glob, echo separators): no action; each cost one retry.

Librarian $0.83 a change, 19 of 45 runs ending pending; tester 103 of 105 runs "nothing" at no cost (no Docker here). Ledger corrections: 10. The history kept through two rebuilds (the store of the previous review held).

The previous review's changes made at once held: the cost store (history intact after both rebuilds), one cost per stretch (later entries read "counted in the first entry"), `Corrects: none` refused.

  1. Claims made without checking, the host's included: the release entry from the plan, a branch deleted as merged on the strength of its conflict markers (`devhost/ontology-guard`, restored), guards believed to run from source that ran the installed copy. Changed now: in a repository carrying `src/devhost`, the `devhost-verify` entrypoint runs the source guards, test `test_the_entrypoint_runs_the_source_guards_in_the_devhost_repository` (`verify-source-guards`, devhost 1.7.2); new item `branch-merged-check`.

  8. The stop route untested on the workstation: no new item; `devcontainer-stop-try` holds it.
  9. Models: no tier moved. The cons' cost per run doubled on judgment-heavy writing reviewed section by section; the backs stay cheap.
  10. One-retry friction (scripted edits on wrapped lines, git dates in another time zone than the ledger, an empty folder the Windows side would not release): no action, each cost one retry.

### 2026-10-21 dc:start and dc:down aliases; idempotent stop via a new running check

- **Session:** con general-purpose (adc2602157cf769ee)
- **Engineer:** operator
- **Branch:** main
- **Asked:** Add `dc:start` and `dc:down` as aliases of `up` and `stop`, make `stop` exit 0 with no container running, add a `running` subcommand to both host paths, and bump devcontainer-cli to 1.4.0.
- **Skill:** none
- **Came out:** Commit 9979628 on `feature/dc-start-stop-aliases/1`, not pushed, author devhost agent, signed off. Changed: `bootstrap/devcontainer.yml`, `bootstrap/host/DevcontainerCli.psm1`, `src/devcontainer-cli/devcontainer-cli.sh`, `src/devcontainer-cli/NOTES.md`, `src/devcontainer-cli/README.md` (regenerated), `src/devcontainer-cli/devcontainer-feature.json` (1.4.0), and `TODO.md` (item `dc-start-stop-aliases`). `task lint` and `verify.py` pass. Real runs used a fake engine; a real engine was not used.
- **Friction:** [tooling] `err=$(...)` aborted under the task shell's errexit; fixed with `|| rc=$?`. [tooling] A command-line `CMD=` leaked into the `up` that runs before exec; fixed so CMD is used only for exec. [tooling] go-task `defer` with `.EXIT_CODE` does not fire for a failed task call, so a line before the check now says a failure refuses the stop. [tooling] The rebuild stop is a shell re-entry rather than `ignore_error`, because the line must be conditional. [tooling] "Task ... _stop is up to date" prints when nothing runs.
- **Learned:** `running` exits 0, 1 or 2, so an engine error fails visibly and does not report "already stopped". The `CMD` leak was only visible once `up` ran before exec.
- **Decisions:** `task dc:start` = `dc:up` and `task dc:down` = `dc:stop`; both idempotent, so other tasks can require them. `claude`, `shell`, `pwsh` and `exec` bring the container up first. `rebuild` stops it first, through the shutdown check. `FORCE=1` skips the check.
- **For the repository:** [tooling] Exercise the PowerShell `running` path at runtime, and run against a real engine, before the 1.4.0 release. Neither was tested here.
- **Decisions needed:** Push and open a PR for `feature/dc-start-stop-aliases/1`, which is not pushed. Starting the devcontainer-cli 1.4.0 release, once merged, is the Operator's call.
- **Measures:** $0.78 (claude-sonnet-5-5 $0.78; 20 replies, 1,460,769 tokens in, 250 out); list-price estimate (this stretch; model haiku wrote the entry)

[docs] The proposal's example `task agent -- host AGENTS_FILE=...` does not work, because anything after `--` is a CLI argument. - **Learned:** Namespace default tasks let `task agents -- <name>` work inside the container. Bash, awk and sed are present on every base image, so the agents YAML subset is parsed in awk, not python3 or jq.
